import {
  hashKey,
  matchQuery,
  notifyManager,
  partialMatchKey,
} from '@tanstack/query-core'
import type {
  Query,
  QueryClient,
  QueryFilters,
  QueryFunctionContext,
  QueryKey,
  QueryState,
} from '@tanstack/query-core'

export interface PersistedQuery {
  buster: string
  queryHash: string
  queryKey: QueryKey
  state: QueryState
}

export type MaybePromise<T> = T | Promise<T>

export interface AsyncStorage<TStorageValue = string> {
  getItem: (key: string) => MaybePromise<TStorageValue | undefined | null>
  setItem: (key: string, value: TStorageValue) => MaybePromise<unknown>
  removeItem: (key: string) => MaybePromise<void>
  entries?: () => MaybePromise<Array<[key: string, value: TStorageValue]>>
}

export interface StoragePersisterOptions<TStorageValue = string> {
  /** The storage client used for setting and retrieving items from cache.
   * For SSR pass in `undefined`.
   */
  storage: AsyncStorage<TStorageValue> | undefined | null
  /**
   * How to serialize the data to storage.
   * @default `JSON.stringify`
   */
  serialize?: (persistedQuery: PersistedQuery) => MaybePromise<TStorageValue>
  /**
   * How to deserialize the data from storage.
   * @default `JSON.parse`
   */
  deserialize?: (cachedString: TStorageValue) => MaybePromise<PersistedQuery>
  /**
   * A unique string that can be used to forcefully invalidate existing caches,
   * if they do not share the same buster string
   */
  buster?: string
  /**
   * The max-allowed age of the cache in milliseconds.
   * If a persisted cache is found that is older than this
   * time, it will be discarded
   * @default 24 hours
   */
  maxAge?: number
  /**
   * Prefix to be used for storage key.
   * Storage key is a combination of prefix and query hash in a form of `prefix-queryHash`.
   * @default 'tanstack-query'
   */
  prefix?: string
  /**
   * If set to `true`, the query will refetch on successful query restoration if the data is stale.
   * If set to `false`, the query will not refetch on successful query restoration.
   * If set to `'always'`, the query will always refetch on successful query restoration.
   * Defaults to `true`.
   */
  refetchOnRestore?: boolean | 'always'
  /**
   * Filters to narrow down which Queries should be persisted.
   */
  filters?: QueryFilters
}

export const PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX = 'tanstack-query'

/**
 * Warning: experimental feature.
 * This utility function enables fine-grained query persistence.
 * Simple add it as a `persister` parameter to `useQuery` or `defaultOptions` on `queryClient`.
 *
 * ```
 * useQuery({
     queryKey: ['myKey'],
     queryFn: fetcher,
     persister: createPersister({
       storage: localStorage,
     }),
   })
   ```
 */
export function experimental_createQueryPersister<TStorageValue = string>({
  storage,
  buster = '',
  maxAge = 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24,
  serialize = JSON.stringify as Required<
    StoragePersisterOptions<TStorageValue>
  >['serialize'],
  deserialize = JSON.parse as Required<
    StoragePersisterOptions<TStorageValue>
  >['deserialize'],
  prefix = PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX,
  refetchOnRestore = true,
  filters,
}: StoragePersisterOptions<TStorageValue>) {
  function isExpiredOrBusted(persistedQuery: PersistedQuery) {
    if (persistedQuery.state.dataUpdatedAt) {
      const queryAge = Date.now() - persistedQuery.state.dataUpdatedAt
      const expired = queryAge > maxAge
      const busted = persistedQuery.buster !== buster

      if (expired || busted) {
        return true
      }

      return false
    }

    return true
  }

  async function retrieveQuery<T>(
    queryHash: string,
    afterRestoreMacroTask?: (persistedQuery: PersistedQuery) => void,
  ) {
    if (storage != null) {
      const storageKey = `${prefix}-${queryHash}`
      try {
        const storedData = await storage.getItem(storageKey)
        if (storedData) {
          let persistedQuery: PersistedQuery
          try {
            persistedQuery = await deserialize(storedData)
          } catch {
            await storage.removeItem(storageKey)
            return
          }

          if (isExpiredOrBusted(persistedQuery)) {
            await storage.removeItem(storageKey)
          } else {
            if (afterRestoreMacroTask) {
              // Just after restoring we want to get fresh data from the server if it's stale
              notifyManager.schedule(() =>
                afterRestoreMacroTask(persistedQuery),
              )
            }
            // We must resolve the promise here, as otherwise we will have `loading` state in the app until `queryFn` resolves
            return persistedQuery.state.data as T
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
          console.error(err)
          console.warn(
            'Encountered an error attempting to restore query cache from persisted location.',
          )
        }
        await storage.removeItem(storageKey)
      }
    }

    return
  }

  async function persistQueryByKey(
    queryKey: QueryKey,
    queryClient: QueryClient,
  ) {
    if (storage != null) {
      const query = queryClient.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey })
      if (query) {
        await persistQuery(query)
      } else {
        if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
          console.warn(
            'Could not find query to be persisted. QueryKey:',
            JSON.stringify(queryKey),
          )
        }
      }
    }
  }

  async function persistQuery(query: Query) {
    if (storage != null) {
      const storageKey = `${prefix}-${query.queryHash}`
      storage.setItem(
        storageKey,
        await serialize({
          state: query.state,
          queryKey: query.queryKey,
          queryHash: query.queryHash,
          buster: buster,
        }),
      )
    }
  }

  async function persisterFn<T, TQueryKey extends QueryKey>(
    queryFn: (context: QueryFunctionContext<TQueryKey>) => T | Promise<T>,
    ctx: QueryFunctionContext<TQueryKey>,
    query: Query,
  ) {
    const matchesFilter = filters ? matchQuery(filters, query) : true

    // Try to restore only if we do not have any data in the cache and we have persister defined
    if (matchesFilter && query.state.data === undefined && storage != null) {
      const restoredData = await retrieveQuery(
        query.queryHash,
        (persistedQuery: PersistedQuery) => {
          // Set proper updatedAt, since resolving in the first pass overrides those values
          query.setState({
            dataUpdatedAt: persistedQuery.state.dataUpdatedAt,
            errorUpdatedAt: persistedQuery.state.errorUpdatedAt,
          })

          if (
            refetchOnRestore === 'always' ||
            (refetchOnRestore === true && query.isStale())
          ) {
            query.fetch()
          }
        },
      )

      if (restoredData !== undefined) {
        return Promise.resolve(restoredData as T)
      }
    }

    // If we did not restore, or restoration failed - fetch
    const queryFnResult = await queryFn(ctx)

    if (matchesFilter && storage != null) {
      // Persist if we have storage defined, we use timeout to get proper state to be persisted
      notifyManager.schedule(() => {

END STEP

interface NotifyEventQueryObserverResultsUpdated extends NotifyEvent {
  type: 'observerResultsUpdated'
  query: Query<any, any, any, any>
}

interface NotifyEventQueryObserverOptionsUpdated extends NotifyEvent {
  type: 'observerOptionsUpdated'
  query: Query<any, any, any, any>
  observer: QueryObserver<any, any, any, any, any>
}

export type QueryCacheNotifyEvent =
  | NotifyEventQueryAdded
  | NotifyEventQueryRemoved
  | NotifyEventQueryUpdated
  | NotifyEventQueryObserverAdded
  | NotifyEventQueryObserverRemoved
  | NotifyEventQueryObserverResultsUpdated
  | NotifyEventQueryObserverOptionsUpdated

type QueryCacheListener = (event: QueryCacheNotifyEvent) => void

export interface QueryStore {
  has: (queryHash: string) => boolean
  set: (queryHash: string, query: Query) => void
  get: (queryHash: string) => Query | undefined
  delete: (queryHash: string) => void
  values: () => IterableIterator<Query>
}

// CLASS

export class QueryCache extends Subscribable<QueryCacheListener> {
  #queries: QueryStore

  constructor(public config: QueryCacheConfig = {}) {
    super()
    this.#queries = new Map<string, Query>()
  }

  build<
    TQueryFnData = unknown,
    TError = DefaultError,
    TData = TQueryFnData,
    TQueryKey extends QueryKey = QueryKey,
  >(
    client: QueryClient,
    options: WithRequired<
      QueryOptions<TQueryFnData, TError, TData, TQueryKey>,
      'queryKey'
    >,

END STEP

STEP 58 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && sed -n '110,165p' packages/query-core/src/queryCache.ts
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
    >,
    state?: QueryState<TData, TError>,
  ): Query<TQueryFnData, TError, TData, TQueryKey> {
    const queryKey = options.queryKey
    const queryHash =
      options.queryHash ?? hashQueryKeyByOptions(queryKey, options)
    let query = this.get<TQueryFnData, TError, TData, TQueryKey>(queryHash)

    if (!query) {
      query = new Query({
        client,
        queryKey,
        queryHash,
        options: client.defaultQueryOptions(options),
        state,
        defaultOptions: client.getQueryDefaults(queryKey),
      })
      this.add(query)
    }

    return query
  }

  add(query: Query<any, any, any, any>): void {
    if (!this.#queries.has(query.queryHash)) {
      this.#queries.set(query.queryHash, query)

      this.notify({
        type: 'added',
        query,
      })
    }
  }

  remove(query: Query<any, any, any, any>): void {
    const queryInMap = this.#queries.get(query.queryHash)

    if (queryInMap) {
      query.destroy()

      if (queryInMap === query) {
        this.#queries.delete(query.queryHash)
      }

      this.notify({ type: 'removed', query })
    }
  }

  clear(): void {
    notifyManager.batch(() => {
      this.getAll().forEach((query) => {
        this.remove(query)
      })
    })
  }

379:    restoreQueries,
  }

  async function removeQueries(
    filters: Pick<QueryFilters, 'queryKey' | 'exact'> = {},
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { exact, queryKey } = filters

    if (storage?.entries) {
      const entries = await storage.entries()
      const storageKeyPrefix = `${prefix}-`
      for (const [key, value] of entries) {
        if (key.startsWith(storageKeyPrefix)) {
          if (!queryKey) {
            await storage.removeItem(key)
            continue
          }

          let persistedQuery: PersistedQuery
          try {
            persistedQuery = await deserialize(value)
          } catch {
            await storage.removeItem(key)
            continue
          }

          if (exact) {
            if (persistedQuery.queryHash !== hashKey(queryKey)) {
              continue
            }
          } else if (!partialMatchKey(persistedQuery.queryKey, queryKey)) {
            continue
          }

          await storage.removeItem(key)
        }
      }
    } else if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
      throw new Error(
        'Provided storage does not implement `entries` method. Removal of stored entries is not possible without ability to iterate over storage items.',
      )
    }
  }

  return {
    persisterFn,
    persistQuery,
    persistQueryByKey,
    retrieveQuery,
    persisterGc,
    restoreQueries,
    removeQueries,
  }
}

  async function restoreQueries(
    queryClient: QueryClient,
    filters: Pick<QueryFilters, 'queryKey' | 'exact'> = {},
  ): Promise<void> {
    const { exact, queryKey } = filters

    if (storage?.entries) {

      const entries = await storage.entries()
      for (const [key, value] of entries) {
        if (key.startsWith(storageKeyPrefix)) {
          let persistedQuery: PersistedQuery
          try {
            persistedQuery = await deserialize(value)
          } catch {
            await storage.removeItem(key)
            continue
          }

            await storage.removeItem(key)
            continue
          }

cd /app && cat > packages/query-core/src/__tests__/persisterRestoreResult.test.tsx <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest'
import {
  QueryCache,
  QueryClient,
  QueryObserver,
  createPersisterRestoreResult,
  isPersisterRestoreResult,
  queryKey,
} from '..'
import { createPersisterRestoreResult as createHelper } from '..'
import type { QueryState } from '..'

function state(overrides: Partial<QueryState<any, any>> = {}): QueryState<any, any> {
  return {
    data: undefined,
    dataUpdateCount: 0,
    dataUpdatedAt: 0,
    error: null,
    errorUpdateCount: 0,
    errorUpdatedAt: 0,
    fetchFailureCount: 0,
    fetchFailureReason: null,
    fetchMeta: null,
    isInvalidated: false,
    status: 'pending',
    fetchStatus: 'idle',
    ...overrides,
  }
}

describe('createPersisterRestoreResult', () => {
  let queryClient: QueryClient

  beforeEach(() => {
    queryClient = new QueryClient()
    queryClient.mount()
  })

  test('returns a restored snapshot marker with data and state', () => {
    const snapshot = state({
      data: 'cached',
      status: 'success',
      dataUpdatedAt: 111,
      dataUpdateCount: 3,
    })
    const result = createPersisterRestoreResult({
      data: 'cached',
      state: snapshot,
    })
    expect(result.data).toBe('cached')
    expect(result.state).toBe(snapshot)
    expect(isPersisterRestoreResult(result)).toBe(true)
    expect(isPersisterRestoreResult({ data: 'cached' })).toBe(false)
  })

  test('adopts persisted state when a persister returns a restore result', async () => {
    const onSuccess = vi.fn()
    const onSettled = vi.fn()
    const client = new QueryClient({
      queryCache: new QueryCache({ onSuccess, onSettled }),
    })
    client.mount()
    const key = queryKey()
    const queryFn = vi.fn(() => 'fresh')
    const restoredState = state({
      data: 'cached',
      dataUpdateCount: 7,
      dataUpdatedAt: 111,
      error: 'boom',
      errorUpdateCount: 4,
      errorUpdatedAt: 222,
      fetchFailureCount: 3,
      fetchFailureReason: 'boom',
      fetchMeta: null,
      isInvalidated: true,
      status: 'error',
      fetchStatus: 'idle',
    })

    await client.fetchQuery({
      queryKey: key,
      queryFn,
      persister: () =>
        createPersisterRestoreResult({
          data: 'cached',
          state: restoredState,
        }),
    })

    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    expect(queryFn).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(onSuccess).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(onSettled).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(query.state).toEqual(restoredState)
    expect(query.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
    expect(query.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(query.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(3)
    expect(query.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(222)
    expect(query.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
    expect(query.state.dataUpdateCount).toBe(7)
  })

  test('observer result exposes restored error metadata and isRefetchError', async () => {
    const key = queryKey()
    const restoredState = state({
      data: 'cached',
      dataUpdatedAt: 111,
      error: 'boom',
      errorUpdatedAt: 222,
      fetchFailureCount: 3,
      fetchFailureReason: 'boom',
      isInvalidated: true,
      status: 'error',
    })

    const observer = new QueryObserver(queryClient, {
      queryKey: key,
      queryFn: () => 'fresh',
      persister: () =>
        createPersisterRestoreResult({ data: 'cached', state: restoredState }),
    })

    const unsubscribe = observer.subscribe((result) => {
      expect(result.status).toBe('error')
      expect(result.isRefetchError).toBe(true)
      expect(result.failureCount).toBe(3)
      expect(result.failureReason).toBe('boom')
      expect(result.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(222)
      expect(result.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(111)
      expect(result.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
    })

    await observer.refetch()
    unsubscribe()
  })

  test('restore merges data and error freshness independently', async () => {
    const key = queryKey()
    const client = new QueryClient()
    client.mount()
    await client.prefetchQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' })
    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!

    // live data is newer, persisted error is newer
    query.setState({
      dataUpdatedAt: 1000,
      dataUpdateCount: 5,
      error: 'live-error',
      errorUpdatedAt: 100,
      fetchFailureCount: 1,
      fetchFailureReason: 'live-error',
      status: 'error',
      isInvalidated: true,
    })

    query.restore(
      state({
        data: 'persisted-data',
        dataUpdatedAt: 500,
        dataUpdateCount: 2,
        error: 'persisted-error',
        errorUpdatedAt: 2000,
        fetchFailureCount: 9,
        fetchFailureReason: 'persisted-error',
        isInvalidated: false,
        status: 'error',
      }),
    )

    expect(query.state.data).toBe('live-data')
    expect(query.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(1000)
    expect(query.state.dataUpdateCount).toBe(5)
    expect(query.state.error).toBe('persisted-error')
    expect(query.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(2000)
    expect(query.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(9)
    expect(query.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(query.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
  })

  test('restore keeps newer persisted data even when live error is newer', async () => {
    const key = queryKey()
    const client = new QueryClient()
    client.mount()
    await client.prefetchQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' })
    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!

    query.setState({
      dataUpdatedAt: 100,
      error: 'live-error',
      errorUpdatedAt: 2000,
      fetchFailureCount: 4,
      fetchFailureReason: 'live-error',
      status: 'error',
      isInvalidated: true,
    })

    query.restore(
      state({
        data: 'persisted-data',
        dataUpdatedAt: 5000,
        dataUpdateCount: 2,
        error: 'persisted-error',
        errorUpdatedAt: 100,
        fetchFailureCount: 1,
        fetchFailureReason: 'persisted-error',
        status: 'error',
      }),
    )

    expect(query.state.data).toBe('persisted-data')
    expect(query.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(5000)
    expect(query.state.error).toBe('live-error')
    expect(query.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(2000)
    expect(query.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(4)
    expect(query.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(query.state.isRefetchError).toBe(true)
  })

  test('restore does not trigger cache success callbacks', async () => {
    const onSuccess = vi.fn()
    const onSettled = vi.fn()
    const client = new QueryClient({
      queryCache: new QueryCache({ onSuccess, onSettled }),
    })
    client.mount()
    const key = queryKey()

    client.getQueryCache().build(client, { queryKey: key }).restore(
      state({
        data: 'cached',
        dataUpdatedAt: 111,
        status: 'success',
        dataUpdateCount: 2,
      }),
    )

    expect(onSuccess).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(onSettled).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })
})

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest'
import {
  QueryCache,
  QueryClient,
  QueryObserver,
  createPersisterRestoreResult,
  isPersisterRestoreResult,
  queryKey,
} from '..'
import type { QueryState } from '..'

function state(
  overrides: Partial<QueryState<any, any>> = {},
): QueryState<any, any> {
  return {
    data: undefined,
    dataUpdateCount: 0,
    dataUpdatedAt: 0,
    error: null,
    errorUpdateCount: 0,
    errorUpdatedAt: 0,
    fetchFailureCount: 0,
    fetchFailureReason: null,
    fetchMeta: null,
    isInvalidated: false,
    status: 'pending',
    fetchStatus: 'idle',
    ...overrides,
  }
}

describe('createPersisterRestoreResult', () => {
  let queryClient: QueryClient

  beforeEach(() => {
    queryClient = new QueryClient()
    queryClient.mount()
  })

  test('returns a restored snapshot marker with data and state', () => {
    const snapshot = state({
      data: 'cached',
      status: 'success',
      dataUpdatedAt: 111,
      dataUpdateCount: 3,
    })
    const result = createPersisterRestoreResult({
      data: 'cached',
      state: snapshot,
    })
    expect(result.data).toBe('cached')
    expect(result.state).toBe(snapshot)
    expect(isPersisterRestoreResult(result)).toBe(true)
    expect(isPersisterRestoreResult({ data: 'cached' })).toBe(false)
  })

  test('adopts persisted state when a persister returns a restore result', async () => {
    const onSuccess = vi.fn()
    const onSettled = vi.fn()
    const client = new QueryClient({
      queryCache: new QueryCache({ onSuccess, onSettled }),
    })
    client.mount()
    const key = queryKey()
    const queryFn = vi.fn(() => 'fresh')
    const restoredState = state({
      data: 'cached',
      dataUpdateCount: 7,
      dataUpdatedAt: 111,
      error: 'boom',
      errorUpdateCount: 4,
      errorUpdatedAt: 222,
      fetchFailureCount: 3,
      fetchFailureReason: 'boom',
      fetchMeta: null,
      isInvalidated: true,
      status: 'error',
      fetchStatus: 'idle',
    })

    await client.fetchQuery({
      queryKey: key,
      queryFn,
      persister: () =>
        createPersisterRestoreResult({
          data: 'cached',
          state: restoredState,
        }),
    })

    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    expect(queryFn).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(onSuccess).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(onSettled).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(query.state).toEqual(restoredState)
    expect(query.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
    expect(query.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(query.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(3)
    expect(query.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(222)
    expect(query.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
    expect(query.state.dataUpdateCount).toBe(7)
  })

  test('observer result exposes restored error metadata and isRefetchError', async () => {
    const key = queryKey()
    const restoredState = state({
      data: 'cached',
      dataUpdatedAt: 111,
      error: 'boom',
      errorUpdatedAt: 222,
      fetchFailureCount: 3,
      fetchFailureReason: 'boom',
      isInvalidated: true,
      status: 'error',
    })

    const observer = new QueryObserver(queryClient, {
      queryKey: key,
      queryFn: () => 'fresh',
      persister: () =>
        createPersisterRestoreResult({ data: 'cached', state: restoredState }),
    })

    const unsubscribe = observer.subscribe(() => {})
    await observer.refetch()
    const result = observer.getCurrentResult()
    unsubscribe()

    expect(result.status).toBe('error')
    expect(result.isRefetchError).toBe(true)
    expect(result.failureCount).toBe(3)
    expect(result.failureReason).toBe('boom')
    expect(result.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(222)
    expect(result.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(111)
    expect(result.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
  })

  test('restore merges data and error freshness independently', async () => {

COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/query-persist-client-core/src/createPersister.ts'
s=open(p).read()
old = """        () => {
          if (
            refetchOnRestore === 'always' ||
            (refetchOnRestore === true && query.isStale())
          ) {
            query.fetch()
          }
        },
"""
new = """        (persistedQuery: PersistedQuery) => {
          // Set proper updatedAt, since resolving in the first pass overrides those values
          query.setState({
            dataUpdatedAt: persistedQuery.state.dataUpdatedAt,
            errorUpdatedAt: persistedQuery.state.errorUpdatedAt,
          })

          if (
            refetchOnRestore === 'always' ||
            (refetchOnRestore === true && query.isStale())
          ) {
            query.fetch()
          }
        },
"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > packages/query-persist-client-core/src/__tests__/persisterRestoreResult.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest'
import { QueryClient, hashKey } from '@tanstack/query-core'
import {
  PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX,
  experimental_createQueryPersister,
} from '../createPersister'
import type { QueryKey } from '@tanstack/query-core'
import type { StoragePersisterOptions } from '../createPersister'

function getFreshStorage() {
  const storage = new Map()
  return {
    getItem: (key: string) => Promise.resolve(storage.get(key)),
    setItem: (key: string, value: unknown) => {
      storage.set(key, value)
      return Promise.resolve()
    },
    removeItem: (key: string) => {
      storage.delete(key)
      return Promise.resolve()
    },
    entries: () => Promise.resolve(Array.from(storage.entries())),
  }
}

function setupPersister(
  queryKey: QueryKey,
  persisterOptions: StoragePersisterOptions,
) {
  const client = new QueryClient()
  const queryHash = hashKey(queryKey)
  const storageKey = `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${queryHash}`
  const persister = experimental_createQueryPersister(persisterOptions)
  return { client, persister, queryHash, queryKey, storageKey }
}

function persistedQuery(queryKey: QueryKey, state: any) {
  return JSON.stringify({
    buster: '',
    queryHash: hashKey(queryKey),
    queryKey,
    state,
  })
}

const errorState = (data: any, dataUpdatedAt: number, error: string, errorUpdatedAt: number) => ({
  data,
  dataUpdateCount: 5,
  dataUpdatedAt,
  error: new Error(error),
  errorUpdateCount: 3,
  errorUpdatedAt,
  fetchFailureCount: 2,
  fetchFailureReason: new Error(error),
  fetchMeta: null,
  isInvalidated: true,
  status: 'error',
  fetchStatus: 'idle',
})

describe('persister restore result', () => {
  beforeAll(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers()
  })

  afterAll(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers()
  })

  test('prefetchQuery with fine-grained persister restores full query state', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, storageKey } = setupPersister(['foo'], { storage })
    const dataUpdatedAt = Date.now()
    const errorUpdatedAt = Date.now()

    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(['foo'], errorState('cached', dataUpdatedAt, 'boom', errorUpdatedAt)),
    )

    const queryFn = vi.fn(() => 'fresh')
    await client.prefetchQuery({
      queryKey: ['foo'],
      queryFn,
      persister: experimental_createQueryPersister({ storage }).persisterFn as any,
    })

    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: ['foo'] })!
    expect(queryFn).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(query.state.data).toBe('cached')
    expect(query.state.error?.message).toBe('boom')
    expect(query.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(query.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(dataUpdatedAt)
    expect(query.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(errorUpdatedAt)
    expect(query.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
    expect(query.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(query.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
  })

  test('bulk restore preserves full state for multiple queries', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, queryKey: key1 } = setupPersister(['foo'], { storage })
    const key2: QueryKey = ['bar']

    const state1 = errorState('foo-data', Date.now(), 'foo-error', Date.now())
    const state2 = {
      data: { pages: ['bar-page1', 'bar-page2'], pageParams: [0, 10] },
      dataUpdateCount: 2,
      dataUpdatedAt: Date.now(),
      error: null,
      errorUpdateCount: 0,
      errorUpdatedAt: 0,
      fetchFailureCount: 0,
      fetchFailureReason: null,
      fetchMeta: null,
      isInvalidated: false,
      status: 'success',
      fetchStatus: 'idle',
    }
    await storage.setItem(
      `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hashKey(key1)}`,
      persistedQuery(key1, state1),
    )
    await storage.setItem(
      `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hashKey(key2)}`,
      persistedQuery(key2, state2),
    )

    await experimental_createQueryPersister({ storage }).restoreQueries(client)

    const q1 = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key1 })!
    expect(q1.state.data).toBe('foo-data')
    expect(q1.state.error?.message).toBe('foo-error')
    expect(q1.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(q1.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(q1.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
    expect(q1.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')

    const q2 = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key2 })!
    expect(q2.state.data).toEqual({
      pages: ['bar-page1', 'bar-page2'],
      pageParams: [0, 10],
    })
    expect(q2.state.status).toBe('success')
  })

  test('bulk restore merges newer live data with newer persisted error metadata', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, queryKey, storageKey } = setupPersister(['merge'], { storage })
    const key = queryKey

    // Seed live cache with newer data
    await client.prefetchQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' })
    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    query.setState({
      dataUpdatedAt: 1000,
      dataUpdateCount: 5,
      error: new Error('live-error'),
      errorUpdatedAt: 100,
      fetchFailureCount: 1,
      fetchFailureReason: new Error('live-error'),
      status: 'error',
      isInvalidated: true,
    })

    // Persisted snapshot has older data but newer error metadata
    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(key, errorState('persisted-data', 500, 'persisted-error', 2000)),
    )

    await experimental_createQueryPersister({ storage }).restoreQueries(client)

    const restored = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    expect(restored.state.data).toBe('live-data')
    expect(restored.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(1000)
    expect(restored.state.error?.message).toBe('persisted-error')
    expect(restored.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(2000)
    expect(restored.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(restored.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(restored.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)

    // observer should see a refetch error
    const observer = new (await import('@tanstack/query-core')).QueryObserver(client, {
      queryKey: key,
    })
    const result = observer.getOptimisticResult(
      client.defaultQueryOptions({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' }),
    )
    expect(result.isRefetchError).toBe(true)
    expect(result.failureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(result.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(2000)
  })

  test('bulk restore keeps newer persisted data when live error is newer', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, queryKey, storageKey } = setupPersister(['merge2'], { storage })
    const key = queryKey

    await client.prefetchQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' })
    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    query.setState({
      dataUpdatedAt: 100,
      error: new Error('live-error'),
      errorUpdatedAt: 2000,
      fetchFailureCount: 4,
      fetchFailureReason: new Error('live-error'),
      status: 'error',
      isInvalidated: true,
    })

    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(key, errorState('persisted-data', 5000, 'persisted-error', 100)),
    )

    await experimental_createQueryPersister({ storage }).restoreQueries(client)

    const restored = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    expect(restored.state.data).toBe('persisted-data')
    expect(restored.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(5000)
    expect(restored.state.error?.message).toBe('live-error')
    expect(restored.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(2000)
    expect(restored.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(4)
    expect(restored.state.status).toBe('error')
  })
})
EOF
echo done
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
done

import { describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest'
import { QueryClient, QueryObserver, hashKey } from '@tanstack/query-core'
import {
  PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX,
  experimental_createQueryPersister,
} from '../createPersister'
import type { QueryKey } from '@tanstack/query-core'
import type { StoragePersisterOptions } from '../createPersister'

function getFreshStorage() {
  const storage = new Map()
  return {
    getItem: (key: string) => Promise.resolve(storage.get(key)),
    setItem: (key: string, value: unknown) => {
      storage.set(key, value)
      return Promise.resolve()
    },
    removeItem: (key: string) => {
      storage.delete(key)
      return Promise.resolve()
    },
    entries: () => Promise.resolve(Array.from(storage.entries())),
  }
}

function setupPersister(
  queryKey: QueryKey,
  persisterOptions: StoragePersisterOptions,
) {
  const client = new QueryClient()
  const queryHash = hashKey(queryKey)
  const storageKey = `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${queryHash}`
  const persister = experimental_createQueryPersister(persisterOptions)
  return { client, persister, queryHash, queryKey, storageKey }
}

function persistedQuery(queryKey: QueryKey, state: any) {
  return JSON.stringify({
    buster: '',
    queryHash: hashKey(queryKey),
    queryKey,
    state,
  })
}

function errorState(
  data: any,
  dataUpdatedAt: number,
  error: string,
  errorUpdatedAt: number,
) {
  return {
    data,
    dataUpdateCount: 5,
    dataUpdatedAt,
    error: new Error(error),
    errorUpdateCount: 3,
    errorUpdatedAt,
    fetchFailureCount: 2,
    fetchFailureReason: new Error(error),
    fetchMeta: null,
    isInvalidated: true,
    status: 'error',
    fetchStatus: 'idle',
  }
}

describe('persister restore result', () => {
  test('prefetchQuery with fine-grained persister restores full query state', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, persister, storageKey } = setupPersister(['foo'], {
      storage,
      refetchOnRestore: false,
    })
    const dataUpdatedAt = Date.now()
    const errorUpdatedAt = Date.now()

    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(
        ['foo'],
        errorState('cached', dataUpdatedAt, 'boom', errorUpdatedAt),
      ),
    )

    const queryFn = vi.fn(() => 'fresh')
    await client.prefetchQuery({
      queryKey: ['foo'],
      queryFn,
      persister: persister.persisterFn,
    })

    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: ['foo'] })!
    expect(queryFn).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(query.state.data).toBe('cached')
    expect(query.state.error?.message).toBe('boom')
    expect(query.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(query.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(dataUpdatedAt)
    expect(query.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(errorUpdatedAt)
    expect(query.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
    expect(query.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(query.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
  })

  test('bulk restore preserves full state for multiple queries', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, persister, queryKey: key1 } = setupPersister(['foo'], {
      storage,
    })
    const key2: QueryKey = ['bar']

    const state1 = errorState('foo-data', Date.now(), 'foo-error', Date.now())
    const state2 = {
      data: { pages: ['bar-page1', 'bar-page2'], pageParams: [0, 10] },
      dataUpdateCount: 2,
      dataUpdatedAt: Date.now(),
      error: null,
      errorUpdateCount: 0,
      errorUpdatedAt: 0,
      fetchFailureCount: 0,
      fetchFailureReason: null,
      fetchMeta: null,
      isInvalidated: false,
      status: 'success',
      fetchStatus: 'idle',
    }
    await storage.setItem(
      `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hashKey(key1)}`,
      persistedQuery(key1, state1),
    )
    await storage.setItem(
      `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hashKey(key2)}`,
      persistedQuery(key2, state2),
    )

    await persister.restoreQueries(client)

    const q1 = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key1 })!
    expect(q1.state.data).toBe('foo-data')
    expect(q1.state.error?.message).toBe('foo-error')
    expect(q1.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(q1.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(q1.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
    expect(q1.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')

    const q2 = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key2 })!
    expect(q2.state.data).toEqual({
      pages: ['bar-page1', 'bar-page2'],
      pageParams: [0, 10],
    })
    expect(q2.state.status).toBe('success')
  })

  test('bulk restore merges newer live data with newer persisted error metadata', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, persister, queryKey, storageKey } = setupPersister(
      ['merge'],
      { storage },
    )
    const key = queryKey

    await client.prefetchQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' })
    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    query.setState({
      dataUpdatedAt: 1000,
      dataUpdateCount: 5,
      error: new Error('live-error'),
      errorUpdatedAt: 100,
      fetchFailureCount: 1,
      fetchFailureReason: new Error('live-error'),
      status: 'error',
      isInvalidated: true,
    })

    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(
        key,
        errorState('persisted-data', 500, 'persisted-error', 2000),
      ),
    )

    await persister.restoreQueries(client)

    const restored = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    expect(restored.state.data).toBe('live-data')
    expect(restored.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(1000)
    expect(restored.state.error?.message).toBe('persisted-error')
    expect(restored.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(2000)
    expect(restored.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(restored.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(restored.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)

    const observer = new QueryObserver(client, {
      queryKey: key,
      queryFn: () => 'live-data',
    })
    const unsubscribe = observer.subscribe(() => {})
    const result = observer.getCurrentResult()
    unsubscribe()
    expect(result.isRefetchError).toBe(true)
    expect(result.failureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(result.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(2000)
  })

  test('bulk restore keeps newer persisted data when live error is newer', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, persister, queryKey, storageKey } = setupPersister(
      ['merge2'],
      { storage },
    )
    const key = queryKey

    await client.prefetchQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' })
    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    query.setState({
      dataUpdatedAt: 100,
      error: new Error('live-error'),
      errorUpdatedAt: 2000,
      fetchFailureCount: 4,
      fetchFailureReason: new Error('live-error'),
      status: 'error',
      isInvalidated: true,
    })

    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(
        key,
        errorState('persisted-data', 5000, 'persisted-error', 100),
      ),
    )

    await persister.restoreQueries(client)

    const restored = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    expect(restored.state.data).toBe('persisted-data')
    expect(restored.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(5000)
    expect(restored.state.error?.message).toBe('live-error')
    expect(restored.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(2000)
    expect(restored.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(4)
    expect(restored.state.status).toBe('error')
  })
})

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > packages/query-persist-client-core/src/__tests__/persisterRestoreResult.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest'
import { QueryClient, QueryObserver, hashKey } from '@tanstack/query-core'
import {
  PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX,
  experimental_createQueryPersister,
} from '../createPersister'
import type { QueryKey } from '@tanstack/query-core'
import type { StoragePersisterOptions } from '../createPersister'

function getFreshStorage() {
  const storage = new Map()
  return {
    getItem: (key: string) => Promise.resolve(storage.get(key)),
    setItem: (key: string, value: unknown) => {
      storage.set(key, value)
      return Promise.resolve()
    },
    removeItem: (key: string) => {
      storage.delete(key)
      return Promise.resolve()
    },
    entries: () => Promise.resolve(Array.from(storage.entries())),
  }
}

function setupPersister(
  queryKey: QueryKey,
  persisterOptions: StoragePersisterOptions,
) {
  const client = new QueryClient()
  const queryHash = hashKey(queryKey)
  const storageKey = `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${queryHash}`
  const persister = experimental_createQueryPersister(persisterOptions)
  return { client, persister, queryHash, queryKey, storageKey }
}

function persistedQuery(queryKey: QueryKey, state: any) {
  return JSON.stringify({
    buster: '',
    queryHash: hashKey(queryKey),
    queryKey,
    state,
  })
}

function errorState(
  data: any,
  dataUpdatedAt: number,
  error: string,
  errorUpdatedAt: number,
) {
  return {
    data,
    dataUpdateCount: 5,
    dataUpdatedAt,
    error: { message: error },
    errorUpdateCount: 3,
    errorUpdatedAt,
    fetchFailureCount: 2,
    fetchFailureReason: { message: error },
    fetchMeta: null,
    isInvalidated: true,
    status: 'error',
    fetchStatus: 'idle',
  }
}

describe('persister restore result', () => {
  test('prefetchQuery with fine-grained persister restores full query state', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, persister, storageKey } = setupPersister(['foo'], {
      storage,
      refetchOnRestore: false,
    })
    const dataUpdatedAt = Date.now()
    const errorUpdatedAt = Date.now()

    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(
        ['foo'],
        errorState('cached', dataUpdatedAt, 'boom', errorUpdatedAt),
      ),
    )

    const queryFn = vi.fn(() => 'fresh')
    await client.prefetchQuery({
      queryKey: ['foo'],
      queryFn,
      persister: persister.persisterFn as any,
    })

    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: ['foo'] })!
    expect(queryFn).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(query.state.data).toBe('cached')
    expect(query.state.error?.message).toBe('boom')
    expect(query.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(query.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(dataUpdatedAt)
    expect(query.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(errorUpdatedAt)
    expect(query.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
    expect(query.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(query.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
  })

  test('bulk restore preserves full state for multiple queries', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, persister, queryKey: key1 } = setupPersister(['foo'], {
      storage,
    })
    const key2: QueryKey = ['bar']
    const now = Date.now()

    const state1 = errorState('foo-data', now, 'foo-error', now)
    const state2 = {
      data: { pages: ['bar-page1', 'bar-page2'], pageParams: [0, 10] },
      dataUpdateCount: 2,
      dataUpdatedAt: now,
      error: null,
      errorUpdateCount: 0,
      errorUpdatedAt: 0,
      fetchFailureCount: 0,
      fetchFailureReason: null,
      fetchMeta: null,
      isInvalidated: false,
      status: 'success',
      fetchStatus: 'idle',
    }
    await storage.setItem(
      `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hashKey(key1)}`,
      persistedQuery(key1, state1),
    )
    await storage.setItem(
      `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hashKey(key2)}`,
      persistedQuery(key2, state2),
    )

    await persister.restoreQueries(client)

    const q1 = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key1 })!
    expect(q1.state.data).toBe('foo-data')
    expect(q1.state.error?.message).toBe('foo-error')
    expect(q1.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(q1.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(q1.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)
    expect(q1.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')

    const q2 = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key2 })!
    expect(q2.state.data).toEqual({
      pages: ['bar-page1', 'bar-page2'],
      pageParams: [0, 10],
    })
    expect(q2.state.status).toBe('success')
  })

  test('bulk restore merges newer live data with newer persisted error metadata', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, persister, queryKey, storageKey } = setupPersister(
      ['merge'],
      { storage },
    )
    const key = queryKey
    const now = Date.now()

    await client.prefetchQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' })
    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    query.setState({
      dataUpdatedAt: now - 1000,
      dataUpdateCount: 5,
      error: { message: 'live-error' },
      errorUpdatedAt: now - 5000,
      fetchFailureCount: 1,
      fetchFailureReason: { message: 'live-error' },
      status: 'error',
      isInvalidated: true,
    })

    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(
        key,
        errorState('persisted-data', now - 2000, 'persisted-error', now - 1000),
      ),
    )

    await persister.restoreQueries(client)

    const restored = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    expect(restored.state.data).toBe('live-data')
    expect(restored.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(now - 1000)
    expect(restored.state.error?.message).toBe('persisted-error')
    expect(restored.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(now - 1000)
    expect(restored.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(restored.state.status).toBe('error')
    expect(restored.state.isInvalidated).toBe(true)

    const observer = new QueryObserver(client, {
      queryKey: key,
      queryFn: () => 'live-data',
    })
    const result = observer.getOptimisticResult(
      client.defaultQueryOptions({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' }),
    )
    expect(result.isRefetchError).toBe(true)
    expect(result.failureCount).toBe(2)
    expect(result.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(now - 1000)
  })

  test('bulk restore keeps newer persisted data when live error is newer', async () => {
    const storage = getFreshStorage()
    const { client, persister, queryKey, storageKey } = setupPersister(
      ['merge2'],
      { storage },
    )
    const key = queryKey
    const now = Date.now()

    await client.prefetchQuery({ queryKey: key, queryFn: () => 'live-data' })
    const query = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    query.setState({
      dataUpdatedAt: now - 5000,
      error: { message: 'live-error' },
      errorUpdatedAt: now - 1000,
      fetchFailureCount: 4,
      fetchFailureReason: { message: 'live-error' },
      status: 'error',
      isInvalidated: true,
    })

    await storage.setItem(
      storageKey,
      persistedQuery(
        key,
        errorState('persisted-data', now - 1000, 'persisted-error', now - 5000),
      ),
    )

    await persister.restoreQueries(client)

    const restored = client.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey: key })!
    expect(restored.state.data).toBe('persisted-data')
    expect(restored.state.dataUpdatedAt).toBe(now - 1000)
    expect(restored.state.error?.message).toBe('live-error')
    expect(restored.state.errorUpdatedAt).toBe(now - 1000)
    expect(restored.state.fetchFailureCount).toBe(4)
    expect(restored.state.status).toBe('error')
  })
})

cd /app && cat packages/react-query/src/__tests__/fine-grained-persister.test.tsx
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import * as React from 'react'
import {
  PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX,
  experimental_createQueryPersister,
} from '@tanstack/query-persist-client-core'
import { queryKey, sleep } from '@tanstack/query-test-utils'
import { QueryCache, QueryClient, hashKey, useQuery } from '..'
import { renderWithClient } from './utils'

describe('fine grained persister', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers()
  })

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers()
  })

  const queryCache = new QueryCache()
  const queryClient = new QueryClient({ queryCache })

  it('should restore query state from persister and not refetch', async () => {
    const key = queryKey()
    const hash = hashKey(key)
    const spy = vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve('Works from queryFn'))

    const mapStorage = new Map()
    const storage = {
      getItem: (itemKey: string) => Promise.resolve(mapStorage.get(itemKey)),
      setItem: (itemKey: string, value: unknown) => {
        mapStorage.set(itemKey, value)
        return Promise.resolve()
      },
      removeItem: (itemKey: string) => {
        mapStorage.delete(itemKey)
        return Promise.resolve()
      },
    }

    await storage.setItem(
      `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hash}`,
      JSON.stringify({
        buster: '',
        queryHash: hash,
        queryKey: key,
        state: {
          dataUpdatedAt: Date.now(),
          data: 'Works from persister',
        },
      }),
    )

    function Test() {
      const [_, setRef] = React.useState<HTMLDivElement | null>()

      const { data } = useQuery({
        queryKey: key,
        queryFn: spy,
        persister: experimental_createQueryPersister({
          storage,
        }).persisterFn,
        staleTime: 5000,
      })

      return <div ref={(value) => setRef(value)}>{data}</div>
    }

    const rendered = renderWithClient(queryClient, <Test />)

    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0)
    expect(rendered.getByText('Works from persister')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(spy).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('should restore query state from persister and refetch', async () => {
    const key = queryKey()
    const hash = hashKey(key)
    const spy = vi.fn(async () => {
      await sleep(5)

      return 'Works from queryFn'
    })

    const mapStorage = new Map()
    const storage = {
      getItem: (itemKey: string) => Promise.resolve(mapStorage.get(itemKey)),
      setItem: (itemKey: string, value: unknown) => {
        mapStorage.set(itemKey, value)
        return Promise.resolve()
      },
      removeItem: (itemKey: string) => {
        mapStorage.delete(itemKey)
        return Promise.resolve()
      },
    }

    await storage.setItem(
      `${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hash}`,
      JSON.stringify({
        buster: '',
        queryHash: hash,
        queryKey: key,
        state: {
          dataUpdatedAt: Date.now(),
          data: 'Works from persister',
        },
      }),
    )

    function Test() {
      const [_, setRef] = React.useState<HTMLDivElement | null>()

      const { data } = useQuery({
        queryKey: key,
        queryFn: spy,
        persister: experimental_createQueryPersister({
          storage,
        }).persisterFn,
      })

      return <div ref={(value) => setRef(value)}>{data}</div>
    }

    const rendered = renderWithClient(queryClient, <Test />)

    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0)
    expect(rendered.getByText('Works from persister')).toBeInTheDocument()
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(6)
    expect(rendered.getByText('Works from queryFn')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  })

  it('should store query state to persister after fetch', async () => {
    const key = queryKey()
    const hash = hashKey(key)
    const spy = vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve('Works from queryFn'))

    const mapStorage = new Map()
    const storage = {
      getItem: (itemKey: string) => Promise.resolve(mapStorage.get(itemKey)),
      setItem: (itemKey: string, value: unknown) => {
        mapStorage.set(itemKey, value)
        return Promise.resolve()
      },
      removeItem: (itemKey: string) => {
        mapStorage.delete(itemKey)
        return Promise.resolve()
      },
    }

    function Test() {
      const [_, setRef] = React.useState<HTMLDivElement | null>()

      const { data } = useQuery({
        queryKey: key,
        queryFn: spy,
        persister: experimental_createQueryPersister({
          storage,
        }).persisterFn,
      })

      return <div ref={(value) => setRef(value)}>{data}</div>
    }

    const rendered = renderWithClient(queryClient, <Test />)

    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0)
    expect(rendered.getByText('Works from queryFn')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)

    const storedItem = await storage.getItem(`${PERSISTER_KEY_PREFIX}-${hash}`)
    expect(JSON.parse(storedItem)).toMatchObject({
      state: {
        data: 'Works from queryFn',
      },
    })
  })
})

END STEP

p='packages/query-core/src/query.ts'
s=open(p).read()
old = """      if (isPersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError>(result)) {
        // A persister signalled that a persisted snapshot was restored instead
        // of being freshly fetched. Adopt the persisted state as the active
        // query state and do not run the normal fetch success callbacks.
        this.restore(result.state)
        return result.data
      }
"""
new = """      if (isPersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError>(result)) {
        // A persister signalled that a persisted snapshot was restored instead
        // of being freshly fetched. Adopt the persisted state as the active
        // query state and do not run the normal fetch success callbacks.
        this.restore({ ...result.state, data: result.data })
        return result.data
      }
"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

+export interface PersisterRestoreResult<

+  data,

+  state: QueryState<TData, TError>

+  return { [persisterRestoreResultSymbol]: true, data, state }
+}
+
+export function isPersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError>(
+  value: unknown,

+  return (
+    typeof value === 'object' &&
+    value !== null &&
+    (value as Record<symbol, unknown>)[persisterRestoreResultSymbol] === true
+  )

     this.#dispatch({ type: 'setState', state, setStateOptions })
   }
 
+  /**
+   * Adopts a persisted query state as the active query state, merging data and
+   * error freshness independently so that newer live data is not discarded just
+   * because the persisted snapshot has newer error metadata (and vice versa).
+   */
+  restore(state: QueryState<TData, TError>): void {
+    const current = this.state
+
+    const useRestoredData =
+      state.data !== undefined &&
+      (current.data === undefined || state.dataUpdatedAt >= current.dataUpdatedAt)
+
+    const data = useRestoredData ? state.data : current.data
+    const dataUpdatedAt = useRestoredData
+      ? state.dataUpdatedAt
+      : current.dataUpdatedAt
+    const dataUpdateCount = useRestoredData
+      ? state.dataUpdateCount
+      : current.dataUpdateCount
+    const fetchMeta = useRestoredData ? state.fetchMeta : current.fetchMeta
+
+    const useRestoredError =
+      state.error !== null &&
+      (current.error === null || state.errorUpdatedAt >= current.errorUpdatedAt)
+
+    const error = useRestoredError ? state.error : current.error
+    const errorUpdatedAt = useRestoredError
+      ? state.errorUpdatedAt
+      : current.errorUpdatedAt
+    const errorUpdateCount = useRestoredError
+      ? state.errorUpdateCount
+      : current.errorUpdateCount
+    const fetchFailureCount = useRestoredError
+      ? state.fetchFailureCount
+      : current.fetchFailureCount
+    const fetchFailureReason = useRestoredError
+      ? state.fetchFailureReason
+      : current.fetchFailureReason
+
+    const status =
+      error !== null ? ('error' as const)
+      : data !== undefined ? ('success' as const)
+      : ('pending' as const)
+
+    const isInvalidated =
+      error !== null || current.isInvalidated || state.isInvalidated
+
+    this.#dispatch({
+      type: 'restore',
+      state: {
+        ...state,
+        data,
+        dataUpdatedAt,
+        dataUpdateCount,
+        fetchMeta,
+        error,
+        errorUpdatedAt,
+        errorUpdateCount,
+        fetchFailureCount,
+        fetchFailureReason,
+        isInvalidated,
+        status,
+        fetchStatus: 'idle',
+      },
+    })
+  }
+

     this.#retryer?.cancel(options)

returncode=0
---
id: createPersister
title: experimental_createQueryPersister
---

## Installation

This utility comes as a separate package and is available under the `'@tanstack/query-persist-client-core'` import.

```bash
npm install @tanstack/query-persist-client-core
```

or

```bash
pnpm add @tanstack/query-persist-client-core
```

or

```bash
yarn add @tanstack/query-persist-client-core
```

or

```bash
bun add @tanstack/query-persist-client-core
```

> Note: This util is also included in the `@tanstack/react-query-persist-client` package, so you do not need to install it separately if you are using that package.

## Usage

- Import the `experimental_createQueryPersister` function
- Create a new `experimental_createQueryPersister`
  - you can pass any `storage` to it that adheres to the `AsyncStorage` interface - the example below uses the async-storage from React Native.
- Pass that `persister` as an option to your Query. This can be done either by passing it to the `defaultOptions` of the `QueryClient` or to any `useQuery` hook instance.
  - If you pass this `persister` as `defaultOptions`, all queries will be persisted to the provided `storage`. You can additionally narrow this down by passing `filters`. In contrast to the `persistClient` plugin, this will not persist the whole query client as a single item, but each query separately. As a key, the query hash is used.
  - If you provide this `persister` to a single `useQuery` hook, only this Query will be persisted.
- Note: `queryClient.setQueryData()` operations are not persisted, this means that if you perform an optimistic update and refresh the page before the query has been invalidated, your changes to the query data will be lost. See https://github.com/TanStack/query/issues/6310

This way, you do not need to store whole `QueryClient`, but choose what is worth to be persisted in your application. Each query is lazily restored (when the Query is first used) and persisted (after each run of the `queryFn`), so it does not need to be throttled. `staleTime` is also respected after restoring the Query, so if data is considered `stale`, it will be refetched immediately after restoring. If data is `fresh`, the `queryFn` will not run.

Garbage collecting a Query from memory **does not** affect the persisted data. That means Queries can be kept in memory for a shorter period of time to be more **memory efficient**. If they are used the next time, they will just be restored from the persistent storage again.

```tsx
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage'
import { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { experimental_createQueryPersister } from '@tanstack/query-persist-client-core'

const persister = experimental_createQueryPersister({
  storage: AsyncStorage,
  maxAge: 1000 * 60 * 60 * 12, // 12 hours
})

const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      gcTime: 1000 * 30, // 30 seconds
      persister: persister.persisterFn,
    },
  },
})
```

### Adapted defaults

The `createPersister` plugin technically wraps the `queryFn`, so it doesn't restore if the `queryFn` doesn't run. In that way, it acts as a caching layer between the Query and the network. Thus, the `networkMode` defaults to `'offlineFirst'` when a persister is used, so that restoring from the persistent storage can also happen even if there is no network connection.

## Additional utilities

Invoking `experimental_createQueryPersister` returns additional utilities in addition to `persisterFn` for easier implementation of userland functionalities.

### `persistQueryByKey(queryKey: QueryKey, queryClient: QueryClient): Promise<void>`

This function will persist `Query` to storage and key defined when creating persister.  
This utility might be used along `setQueryData` to persist optimistic update to storage without waiting for invalidation.

```tsx
const persister = experimental_createQueryPersister({
  storage: AsyncStorage,
  maxAge: 1000 * 60 * 60 * 12, // 12 hours
})

const queryClient = useQueryClient()

useMutation({
  mutationFn: updateTodo,
  onMutate: async (newTodo) => {
    ...
    // Optimistically update to the new value
    queryClient.setQueryData(['todos'], (old) => [...old, newTodo])
    // And persist it to storage
    persister.persistQueryByKey(['todos'], queryClient)
    ...
  },
})
```

### `retrieveQuery<T>(queryHash: string): Promise<T | undefined>`

This function would attempt to retrieve persisted query by `queryHash`.  
If `query` is `expired`, `busted` or `malformed` it would be removed from the storage instead, and `undefined` would be returned.

### `persisterGc(): Promise<void>`

This function can be used to sporadically clean up stoage from `expired`, `busted` or `malformed` entries.

For this function to work, your storage must expose `entries` method that would return a `key-value tuple array`.  
For example `Object.entries(localStorage)` for `localStorage` or `entries` from `idb-keyval`.

### `restoreQueries(queryClient: QueryClient, filters): Promise<void>`

This function can be used to restore queries that are currently stored by persister.  
For example when your app is starting up in offline mode, or you want all or only specific data from previous session to be immediately available without intermediate `loading` state.

The filter object supports the following properties:

- `queryKey?: QueryKey`
  - Set this property to define a query key to match on.
- `exact?: boolean`
  - If you don't want to search queries inclusively by query key, you can pass the `exact: true` option to return only the query with the exact query key you have passed.

For this function to work, your storage must expose `entries` method that would return a `key-value tuple array`.  
For example `Object.entries(localStorage)` for `localStorage` or `entries` from `idb-keyval`.

### `removeQueries(filters): Promise<void>`


END STEP

    65	export interface PersisterRestoreResult<
    66	  TData = unknown,
    67	  TError = DefaultError,
    68	> {
    69	  [persisterRestoreResultSymbol]: true
    70	  data: TData

    74	export function createPersisterRestoreResult<
    75	  TData,
    76	  TError = DefaultError,
    77	>({
    78	  data,
    79	  state,
    80	}: {
    81	  data: TData
    82	  state: QueryState<TData, TError>
    83	}): PersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError> {
    84	  return { [persisterRestoreResultSymbol]: true, data, state }
    85	}
    86	
    87	export function isPersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError>(
    88	  value: unknown,
    89	): value is PersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError> {
    90	  return (
    91	    typeof value === 'object' &&
    92	    value !== null &&
    93	    (value as Record<symbol, unknown>)[persisterRestoreResultSymbol] === true
    94	  )
    95	}
    96	
    97	export interface FetchContext<
    98	  TQueryFnData,
    99	  TError,
   100	  TData,
   101	  TQueryKey extends QueryKey = QueryKey,
   102	> {
   103	  fetchFn: () => unknown | Promise<unknown>
   104	  fetchOptions?: FetchOptions
   105	  signal: AbortSignal
   285	    state: Partial<QueryState<TData, TError>>,
   286	    setStateOptions?: SetStateOptions,
   287	  ): void {
   288	    this.#dispatch({ type: 'setState', state, setStateOptions })
   289	  }
   290	
   291	  /**
   292	   * Adopts a persisted query state as the active query state, merging data and
   293	   * error freshness independently so that newer live data is not discarded just
   294	   * because the persisted snapshot has newer error metadata (and vice versa).
   295	   */
   296	  restore(state: QueryState<TData, TError>): void {
   297	    const current = this.state
   298	
   299	    const useRestoredData =
   300	      state.data !== undefined &&
   301	      (current.data === undefined || state.dataUpdatedAt >= current.dataUpdatedAt)
   302	
   303	    const data = useRestoredData ? state.data : current.data
   304	    const dataUpdatedAt = useRestoredData
   305	      ? state.dataUpdatedAt
   306	      : current.dataUpdatedAt
   307	    const dataUpdateCount = useRestoredData
   308	      ? state.dataUpdateCount
   309	      : current.dataUpdateCount
   310	    const fetchMeta = useRestoredData ? state.fetchMeta : current.fetchMeta
   311	
   312	    const useRestoredError =
   313	      state.error !== null &&
   314	      (current.error === null || state.errorUpdatedAt >= current.errorUpdatedAt)
   315	
   316	    const error = useRestoredError ? state.error : current.error
   317	    const errorUpdatedAt = useRestoredError
   318	      ? state.errorUpdatedAt
   319	      : current.errorUpdatedAt
   320	    const errorUpdateCount = useRestoredError
   321	      ? state.errorUpdateCount
   322	      : current.errorUpdateCount
   323	    const fetchFailureCount = useRestoredError
   324	      ? state.fetchFailureCount
   325	      : current.fetchFailureCount
   326	    const fetchFailureReason = useRestoredError
   327	      ? state.fetchFailureReason
   328	      : current.fetchFailureReason
   329	
   330	    const status =
   331	      error !== null ? ('error' as const)
   332	      : data !== undefined ? ('success' as const)
   333	      : ('pending' as const)
   334	
   335	    const isInvalidated =
   336	      error !== null || current.isInvalidated || state.isInvalidated
   337	
   338	    this.#dispatch({
   339	      type: 'restore',
   340	      state: {
   341	        ...state,
   342	        data,
   343	        dataUpdatedAt,
   344	        dataUpdateCount,
   345	        fetchMeta,
   346	        error,
   347	        errorUpdatedAt,
   348	        errorUpdateCount,
   349	        fetchFailureCount,
   350	        fetchFailureReason,
   351	        isInvalidated,
   352	        status,
   353	        fetchStatus: 'idle',
   354	      },
   355	    })
   655	        this.#dispatch({ type: 'continue' })
   656	      },
   657	      retry: context.options.retry,
   658	      retryDelay: context.options.retryDelay,
   659	      networkMode: context.options.networkMode,
   660	      canRun: () => true,
   661	    })
   662	
   663	    try {
   664	      const result = (await this.#retryer.start()) as
   665	        | TData
   666	        | PersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError>
   667	
   668	      if (isPersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError>(result)) {
   669	        // A persister signalled that a persisted snapshot was restored instead
   670	        // of being freshly fetched. Adopt the persisted state as the active
   671	        // query state and do not run the normal fetch success callbacks.
   672	        this.restore({ ...result.state, data: result.data })
   673	        return result.data
   674	      }
   675	
   676	      const data = result
   677	      if (data === undefined) {
   678	        if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
   679	          console.error(
   680	            `Query data cannot be undefined. Please make sure to return a value other than undefined from your query function. Affected query key: ${this.queryHash}`,
   681	          )
   682	        }
   683	        throw new Error(`${this.queryHash} data is undefined`)
   684	      }
   685	
   686	      this.setData(data)
   687	
   688	      // Notify cache callback
   689	      this.#cache.config.onSuccess?.(data, this as Query<any, any, any, any>)
   690	      this.#cache.config.onSettled?.(
   770	              fetchFailureReason: null,
   771	            }),
   772	          }
   773	          // If fetching ends successfully, we don't need revertState as a fallback anymore.
   774	          // For manual updates, capture the state to revert to it in case of a cancellation.
   775	          this.#revertState = action.manual ? newState : undefined
   776	
   777	          return newState
   778	        case 'restore':
   779	          return {
   780	            ...action.state,
   781	            fetchStatus: 'idle' as const,
   782	          }
   783	        case 'error':
   784	          const error = action.error
   785	          return {
   786	            ...state,
   787	            error,
   788	            errorUpdateCount: state.errorUpdateCount + 1,
   789	            errorUpdatedAt: Date.now(),
   790	            fetchFailureCount: state.fetchFailureCount + 1,

  async function retrieveQuerySnapshot<T>(
    queryHash: string,
    afterRestoreMacroTask?: (persistedQuery: PersistedQuery) => void,
  ): Promise<{ data: T; state: QueryState } | undefined> {
    if (storage != null) {
      const storageKey = `${prefix}-${queryHash}`
      try {
        const storedData = await storage.getItem(storageKey)
        if (storedData) {
          let persistedQuery: PersistedQuery
          try {
            persistedQuery = await deserialize(storedData)
          } catch {
            await storage.removeItem(storageKey)
            return
          }

          if (isExpiredOrBusted(persistedQuery)) {
            await storage.removeItem(storageKey)
          } else {
            if (afterRestoreMacroTask) {
              // Just after restoring we want to get fresh data from the server if it's stale
              notifyManager.schedule(() =>
                afterRestoreMacroTask(persistedQuery),
              )
            }
            // We must resolve the promise here, as otherwise we will have `loading` state in the app until `queryFn` resolves
            return {
              data: persistedQuery.state.data as T,
              state: persistedQuery.state,
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
          console.error(err)
          console.warn(
            'Encountered an error attempting to restore query cache from persisted location.',
          )
        }
        await storage.removeItem(storageKey)
      }
    }

    return
  }

  async function retrieveQuery<T>(
    queryHash: string,
    afterRestoreMacroTask?: (persistedQuery: PersistedQuery) => void,
  ): Promise<T | undefined> {
    return (
      await retrieveQuerySnapshot<T>(queryHash, afterRestoreMacroTask)
    )?.data
  }

  async function persistQueryByKey(
    queryKey: QueryKey,
    queryClient: QueryClient,
  ) {
    if (storage != null) {
      const query = queryClient.getQueryCache().find({ queryKey })
      if (query) {
        await persistQuery(query)
      } else {
        if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
          console.warn(
            'Could not find query to be persisted. QueryKey:',
            JSON.stringify(queryKey),
          )
        }
      }
    }
  }

  async function persistQuery(query: Query) {
    if (storage != null) {
      const storageKey = `${prefix}-${query.queryHash}`
      storage.setItem(
        storageKey,
        await serialize({
          state: query.state,
          queryKey: query.queryKey,
          queryHash: query.queryHash,
          buster: buster,
        }),
      )
    }
  }

  async function persisterFn<T, TQueryKey extends QueryKey>(
    queryFn: (context: QueryFunctionContext<TQueryKey>) => T | Promise<T>,
    ctx: QueryFunctionContext<TQueryKey>,
    query: Query,
  ) {
    const matchesFilter = filters ? matchQuery(filters, query) : true

    // Try to restore only if we do not have any data in the cache and we have persister defined
    if (matchesFilter && query.state.data === undefined && storage != null) {
      const restoredQuery = await retrieveQuerySnapshot<T>(
        query.queryHash,
        (persistedQuery: PersistedQuery) => {
          // Set proper updatedAt, since resolving in the first pass overrides those values
          query.setState({
            dataUpdatedAt: persistedQuery.state.dataUpdatedAt,
            errorUpdatedAt: persistedQuery.state.errorUpdatedAt,
          })

          if (
            refetchOnRestore === 'always' ||
            (refetchOnRestore === true && query.isStale())
          ) {
            query.fetch()
          }
        },
      )

      if (restoredQuery !== undefined) {
        return createPersisterRestoreResult({
          data: restoredQuery.data,
          state: restoredQuery.state,
        })
      }
    }


END STEP

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest'
import {
  QueryCache,
  QueryClient,
  QueryObserver,
  createPersisterRestoreResult,
  isPersisterRestoreResult,
} from '..'
import type { QueryState } from '..'

function state(
  overrides: Partial<QueryState<any, any>> = {},
): QueryState<any, any> {
  return {
    data: undefined,
    dataUpdateCount: 0,
    dataUpdatedAt: 0,
    error: null,
    errorUpdateCount: 0,
    errorUpdatedAt: 0,
    fetchFailureCount: 0,
    fetchFailureReason: null,
    fetchMeta: null,
    isInvalidated: false,
    status: 'pending',
    fetchStatus: 'idle',
    ...overrides,
  }
}

describe('createPersisterRestoreResult', () => {
  let queryClient: QueryClient

  beforeEach(() => {
    queryClient = new QueryClient()
    queryClient.mount()
  })

  test('returns a restored snapshot marker with data and state', () => {
    const snapshot = state({
      data: 'cached',
      status: 'success',
      dataUpdatedAt: 111,
      dataUpdateCount: 3,
    })
    const result = createPersisterRestoreResult({
      data: 'cached',
      state: snapshot,
    })
    expect(result.data).toBe('cached')
    expect(result.state).toBe(snapshot)
    expect(isPersisterRestoreResult(result)).toBe(true)
    expect(isPersisterRestoreResult({ data: 'cached' })).toBe(false)
  })

  test('adopts persisted state when a persister returns a restore result', async () => {
    const onSuccess = vi.fn()
    const onSettled = vi.fn()
    const client = new QueryClient({
      queryCache: new QueryCache({ onSuccess, onSettled }),
    })
    client.mount()
    const key = ['persister-restore']
    const queryFn = vi.fn(() => 'fresh')
    const restoredState = state({
      data: 'cached',
      dataUpdateCount: 7,
      dataUpdatedAt: 111,
      error: new Error('boom'),
      errorUpdateCount: 4,
      errorUpdatedAt: 222,
      fetchFailureCount: 3,
      fetchFailureReason: new Error('boom'),
      fetchMeta: null,
      isInvalidated: true,
      status: 'error',
      fetchStatus: 'idle',
    })

    await client.fetchQuery({

STEP 143 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && git commit -m "fix(query-core): preserve full query state when restoring fine-grained persisted queries

Add createPersisterRestoreResult to query-core so a persister can signal
that a persisted snapshot was restored instead of freshly fetched. When
returned from the persister option, the query adopts the persisted state
as its active state (status, errors, failure counters, timestamps,
invalidation markers and infinite-query pagination) with fetchStatus
idle, and skips normal fetch success callbacks.

experimental_createQueryPersister now returns this marker for lazy
restores and bulk restoreQueries rebuilds the cache from the full
persisted state, merging data and error freshness independently when a
persisted snapshot is restored over an existing live query." && git log --oneline -3

+export const persisterRestoreResultSymbol = Symbol('persisterRestoreResult')
+
+export interface PersisterRestoreResult<
+  TData = unknown,
+  TError = DefaultError,
+> {
+  [persisterRestoreResultSymbol]: true
+  data: TData
+  state: QueryState<TData, TError>
+}
+
+export function createPersisterRestoreResult<
+  TData,
+  TError = DefaultError,
+>({
+  data,
+  state,
+}: {
+  data: TData
+  state: QueryState<TData, TError>
+}): PersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError> {
+  return { [persisterRestoreResultSymbol]: true, data, state }
+}
+
+export function isPersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError>(
+  value: unknown,
+): value is PersisterRestoreResult<TData, TError> {
+  return (
+    typeof value === 'object' &&
+    value !== null &&
+    (value as Record<symbol, unknown>)[persisterRestoreResultSymbol] === true
+  )
+}

     this.#dispatch({ type: 'setState', state, setStateOptions })
   }
 
+  /**
+   * Adopts a persisted query state as the active query state, merging data and
+   * error freshness independently so that newer live data is not discarded just
+   * because the persisted snapshot has newer error metadata (and vice versa).
+   */
+  restore(state: QueryState<TData, TError>): void {
+    const current = this.state
+
+    const useRestoredData =
+      state.data !== undefined &&
+      (current.data === undefined || state.dataUpdatedAt >= current.dataUpdatedAt)
+
+    const data = useRestoredData ? state.data : current.data
+    const dataUpdatedAt = useRestoredData
+      ? state.dataUpdatedAt
+      : current.dataUpdatedAt
+    const dataUpdateCount = useRestoredData
+      ? state.dataUpdateCount
+      : current.dataUpdateCount
+    const fetchMeta = useRestoredData ? state.fetchMeta : current.fetchMeta
+
+    const useRestoredError =
+      state.error !== null &&
+      (current.error === null || state.errorUpdatedAt >= current.errorUpdatedAt)
+
+    const error = useRestoredError ? state.error : current.error
+    const errorUpdatedAt = useRestoredError
+      ? state.errorUpdatedAt
+      : current.errorUpdatedAt
+    const errorUpdateCount = useRestoredError
+      ? state.errorUpdateCount
+      : current.errorUpdateCount
+    const fetchFailureCount = useRestoredError
+      ? state.fetchFailureCount
+      : current.fetchFailureCount
+    const fetchFailureReason = useRestoredError
+      ? state.fetchFailureReason
+      : current.fetchFailureReason
+
+    const status =
+      error !== null ? ('error' as const)
+      : data !== undefined ? ('success' as const)
+      : ('pending' as const)
+
+    const isInvalidated =
+      error !== null || current.isInvalidated || state.isInvalidated
+
+    this.#dispatch({
+      type: 'restore',
+      state: {
+        ...state,
+        data,
+        dataUpdatedAt,
+        dataUpdateCount,
+        fetchMeta,
+        error,
+        errorUpdatedAt,
+        errorUpdateCount,
+        fetchFailureCount,
+        fetchFailureReason,
+        isInvalidated,
+        status,
+        fetchStatus: 'idle',
